Identify key columns (defensive mapping)\n    plan_return_col = pick_col(plan, [\"Return ID\", \"ReturnID\", \"Return Id\"])\n    plan_line_col = pick_col(plan, [\"Line ID\", \"LineID\", \"Line Id\", \"Line\"])\n    plan_planned_disp_col = pick_col(plan, [\"Planned Disposition\", \"PlannedDisposition\", \"Planned Disp\", \"Planned\"])\n    plan_reason_col = pick_col(plan, [\"Reason Code\", \"ReasonCode\", \"Reason\"])\n    plan_qty_col = pick_col(plan, [\"Requested Qty\", \"RequestedQty\", \"Qty\", \"Requested Quantity\"])\n    plan_wh_col = pick_col(plan, [\"Warehouse\", \"WH\", \"Warehouse Code\"])\n    plan_carrier_col = pick_col(plan, [\"Carrier\", \"Ship Carrier\"])\n    plan_lane_col = pick_col(plan, [\"Lane\", \"Shipping Lane\", \"Route\"])\n\n    events_return_col = pick_col(events, [\"Return ID\", \"ReturnID\", \"Return Id\"])\n    events_line_col = pick_col(events, [\"Line ID\", \"LineID\", \"Line Id\", \"Line\"])\n    events_status_col = pick_col(events, [\"Event Status\", \"Status\", \"Disposition Status\"])\n    events_final_disp_col = pick_col(events, [\"Final Disposition\", \"Disposition\", \"FinalDisposition\"])\n\n    # Timestamp/sequence column choice for \"latest\" completed event\n    events_time_col = pick_col(\n        events,\n        [\n            \"Event Time\",\n            \"Event Timestamp\",\n            \"Event Date\",\n            \"Completed Time\",\n            \"Timestamp\",\n            \"Last Updated\",\n            \"Sequence\",\n            \"Seq\",\n        ],\n        required=False,\n    )\n\n    # 2b – Build alias mapping\n    alias_alias_col = pick_col(alias, [\"Alias\", \"Disposition Alias\"])\n    alias_std_col = pick_col(alias, [\"Standard Disposition\", \"Standard\", \"Disposition\"])\n\n    alias_map = {}\n    for _, row in alias.iterrows():\n        a = norm_lower(row[alias_alias_col])\n        s = norm_lower(row[alias_std_col])\n        if a is None or s is None:\n            continue\n        alias_map[a] = s\n\n    print(\"ALIAS mapping (lower->lower):\")\n    for k in sorted(alias_map.keys()):\n        print(f\"  {k!r} -> {alias_map[k]!r}\")\n\n    # 2c – Filter events to COMPLETED only, keep latest per (Return ID, Line ID)\n    status_norm = events[events_status_col].astype(str).str.strip().str.lower()\n    completed = events[status_norm.eq(\"completed\")].copy()\n\n    if events_time_col is not None:\n        # Try to parse time; if it fails, sort as string.\n        parsed = pd.to_datetime(completed[events_time_col], errors=\"coerce\", utc=False)\n        completed[\"__sort_time\"] = parsed\n        # Where parsing failed, fall back to original value to stabilize ordering\n        # (keep as string, but we only use it as a tie-breaker via stable sort).\n        completed[\"__sort_time_str\"] = completed[events_time_col].astype(str)\n        completed = completed.sort_values([\"__sort_time\", \"__sort_time_str\"], ascending=[False, False], kind=\"mergesort\")\n    else:\n        completed = completed.sort_index(ascending=False)\n\n    latest_completed = completed.drop_duplicates(subset=[events_return_col, events_line_col], keep=\"first\").copy()\n\n    # Keep only needed columns for merge\n    latest_completed = latest_completed[[events_return_col, events_line_col, events_final_disp_col]].copy()\n    latest_completed = latest_completed.rename(columns={events_final_disp_col: \"Final Disposition\"})\n\n    # 2d – Build Formatted Data\n    base = plan[[\n        plan_return_col,\n        plan_line_col,\n        plan_planned_disp_col,\n        plan_reason_col,\n        plan_qty_col,\n        plan_wh_col,\n        plan_carrier_col,\n        plan_lane_col,\n    ]].copy()\n\n    base = base.rename(\n        columns={\n            plan_return_col: \"Return ID\",\n            plan_line_col: \"Line ID\",\n            plan_planned_disp_col: \"Planned Disposition\",\n            plan_reason_col: \"Reason Code\",\n            plan_qty_col: \"Requested Qty\",\n            plan_wh_col: \"Warehouse\",\n            plan_carrier_col: \"Carrier\",\n            plan_lane_col: \"Lane\",\n        }\n    )\n\n    formatted = base.merge(latest_completed, how=\"left\", on=[\"Return ID\", \"Line ID\"])\n\n    # Normalize final disposition with alias mapping (lowercase comparisons)\n    formatted[\"__final_lower\"] = formatted[\"Final Disposition\"].apply(norm_lower)\n    formatted[\"Normalized Final Disposition\"] = formatted[\"__final_lower\"].map(alias_map).fillna(formatted[\"__final_lower\"])\n\n    formatted[\"__planned_lower\"] = formatted[\"Planned Disposition\"].apply(norm_lower)\n\n    # Missing Final Event = 1 if no COMPLETED event\n    formatted[\"Missing Final Event\"] = formatted[\"Final Disposition\"].isna().astype(int)\n\n    # Disposition Mismatch: only if final exists AND normalized != planned (lowercased)\n    has_final = formatted[\"Missing Final Event\"].eq(0)\n    mismatch = has_final & (formatted[\"Normalized Final Disposition\"].fillna(\"\") != formatted[\"__planned_lower\"].fillna(\"\"))\n    formatted[\"Disposition Mismatch\"] = mismatch.astype(int)\n\n    formatted[\"Total Errors\"] = (formatted[\"Missing Final Event\"] + formatted[\"Disposition Mismatch\"]).astype(int)\n\n    def error_summary_row(row):\n        miss = int(row[\"Missing Final Event\"]) == 1\n        mis = int(row[\"Disposition Mismatch\"]) == 1\n        if not miss and not mis:\n            return \"None\"\n        if miss and not mis:\n            return \"Missing Final Event\"\n        if not miss and mis:\n            return \"Disposition Mismatch\"\n        return \"Missing Final Event, Disposition Mismatch\"\n\n    formatted[\"Error Summary\"] = formatted.apply(error_summary_row, axis=1)\n\n    formatted_out = formatted[[\n        \"Return ID\",\n        \"Line ID\",\n        \"Planned Disposition\",\n        \"Reason Code\",\n        \"Requested Qty\",\n        \"Warehouse\",\n        \"Carrier\",\n        \"Lane\",\n        \"Final Disposition\",\n        \"Missing Final Event\",\n        \"Disposition Mismatch\",\n        \"Total Errors\",\n        \"Error Summary\",\n    ]].copy()\n\n    # Ensure numeric columns are ints (0/1) and not floats\n    for c in [\"Missing Final Event\", \"Disposition Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\"]:\n        formatted_out[c] = formatted_out[c].astype(int)\n\n    # 2e – Build Summary sheet\n    summary = (\n        formatted_out\n        .groupby([\"Warehouse\", \"Carrier\"], dropna=False)[[\"Missing Final Event\", \"Disposition Mismatch\", \"Total Errors\"]]\n        .sum()\n        .reset_index()\n    )\n\n    summary = summary[summary[\"Total Errors\"] > 0].copy()\n    summary = summary.sort_values([\"Warehouse\", \"Carrier\"], ascending=[True, True], kind=\"mergesort\")\n\n    # Grand total row\n    grand = {\n        \"Warehouse\": \"Grand Total\",\n        \"Carrier\": \"-\",\n        \"Missing Final Event\": int(formatted_out[\"Missing Final Event\"].sum()),\n        \"Disposition Mismatch\": int(formatted_out[\"Disposition Mismatch\"].sum()),\n        \"Total Errors\": int(formatted_out[\"Total Errors\"].sum()),\n    }\n    summary = pd.concat([summary, pd.DataFrame([grand])], ignore_index=True)\n\n    summary_out = summary.rename(\n        columns={\n            \"Missing Final Event\": \"Missing Final Events\",\n            \"Disposition Mismatch\": \"Disposition Mismatches\",\n        }\n    )[[\n        \"Warehouse\",\n        \"Carrier\",\n        \"Missing Final Events\",\n        \"Disposition Mismatches\",\n        \"Total Errors\",\n    ]].copy()\n\n    # 2f – Write Excel\n    out_xlsx = \"/root/Returns_Disposition_Audit.xlsx\"\n    with pd.ExcelWriter(out_xlsx, engine=\"openpyxl\") as writer:\n        plan.to_excel(writer, sheet_name=\"RawData\", index=False)\n        formatted_out.to_excel(writer, sheet_name=\"Formatted Data\", index=False)\n        summary_out.to_excel(writer, sheet_name=\"Summary\", index=False)\n\n    # 2g – Build Word document\n    totals_missing = int(formatted_out[\"Missing Final Event\"].sum())\n    totals_mismatch = int(formatted_out[\"Disposition Mismatch\"].sum())\n    totals_errors = int(formatted_out[\"Total Errors\"].sum())\n\n    top_returns = (\n        formatted_out\n        .groupby(\"Return ID\", dropna=False)[\"Total Errors\"]\n        .sum()\n        .sort_values(ascending=False)\n    )\n    top_return_ids = [str(idx) for idx, val in top_returns.items() if int(val) > 0][:2]\n\n    # If there are fewer than 2 returns with errors, still mention two IDs from the dataset\n    if len(top_return_ids) < 2:\n        all_ids = list(formatted_out[\"Return ID\"].dropna().astype(str).unique())\n        for rid in all_ids:\n            if rid not in top_return_ids:\n                top_return_ids.append(rid)\n            if len(top_return_ids) >= 2:\n                break\n\n    high_priority_sentence = (\n        f\"High-priority returns requiring immediate review include Return ID {top_return_ids[0]} and Return ID {top_return_ids[1]}.\"\n        if len(top_return_ids) >= 2\n        else \"High-priority returns requiring immediate review include the highest-error Return IDs identified in the audit.\"\n    )\n\n    doc = Document()\n    paragraph = (\n        \"This audit compares planned return-line dispositions against the latest recorded completed disposition events. \"\n        \"A 'Missing Final Event' means no completed disposition event was recorded for a return line. \"\n        \"A 'Disposition Mismatch' means the final recorded disposition differs from the originally planned disposition. \"\n        f\"The audit identified {totals_missing} Missing Final Events, {totals_mismatch} Disposition Mismatches, and {totals_errors} Total Errors. \"\n        \"We recommend investigating root causes at the warehouses with the highest error counts and implementing real-time disposition validation to prevent mismatches and ensure events are completed. \"\n        + high_priority_sentence\n    )\n    doc.add_paragraph(paragraph)\n\n    out_docx = \"/root/Returns_Disposition_Brief.docx\"\n    doc.save(out_docx)\n\n    #
